Run 39333825784 (e1bce68) stopped at Decode start-up: every Decode DP rank
raised "Qwen SM90 MegaMoE requires ... DP-local EAGLE3 Decode" because the
image's qwen3-sm90-megamoe patch requires speculative_num_draft_tokens ==
4. The same guard was relaxed to (3, 4) on 2026-10-17 in b996afd after an
audit found no other dependence on four verify rows (forward_megamoe sizes
from hidden_states, the symmetric buffer and the routed run take num_tokens
at run time, the compiled geometry has no draft term, the CUDA graph
runners derive their width from the draft count) and run 39035989837 served
steps 2 / draft 3 with it; that change never reached main.

patches/sglang/megamoe-decode-draft3-guard.patch carries only that line
(== 4 -> in (3, 4)) and is appended to sglang_overlay_patches, so no image
build is needed. It was applied on the extracted qwen3_moe.py of image
317ab7c7 after the two existing overlay patches. Everything else is e1bce68:
main's 8258.34 configuration with EAGLE3 steps 2 / draft tokens 3.
